been run yet because this section only records the diagnostic matrix result.

lane unless owner decision prioritizes a later but more release-critical row.

## Gate66A Stage04 Canonical ACT Slot-Order Repair

Status:
Closed locally pending broad Smoke B, safety scans, commit, push, and CI/Pages.

Symptom:
Gate66 Secularism failed at Stage04 even though the row contained ACT-looking
tokens. The row omitted canonical `::` separators and placed `body_ref=` before
`π=`, so the runner correctly rejected it as non-canonical ACT syntax.

Failed check:
`stage-04 act_rows[0] is not a parseable canonical ACT row` from
`.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-224043-matrix-secularism/records/staged-handoff-failure.json`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage04 producer instructions and
self-test; `tools/check_staged_runtime_handshake.py` canonical ACT row checker;
neutral fixture
`tests/staged-runtime-handshake/invalid/stage04-act-row-missing-slot-separators.json`.

DSL transition protected:
`burden_id -> route/TTP selection -> owner operation -> ACT row -> controlled
delta_result`. This repair protects the ACT-row serialization boundary only:
`body_ref` stays the bare join key, owner/operation stay in the ACT bracket and
typed detail fields, `π` remains pressure, and `Δ` remains the controlled
state-transition result. It does not accept polluted `body_ref` aliases or broad
prose tolerance.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the smoke fail? The model emitted ACT-like rows without canonical
   slot separators/order.
2. Why did the parser reject them? The row did not match the strict ACT grammar
   required for stable Stage04 handoff.
3. Why did the model drift? Producer guidance named required tokens but did not
   explicitly spell the slot grammar/order and forbidden no-separator dialect.
4. Why was this not caught by no-model checks? Existing invalid canaries caught
   missing fields and polluted body_ref forms, but not rows that had every token
   in a non-canonical order/serialization.
5. What prevents recurrence? Add a neutral invalid fixture and runner self-test
   for the exact missing-slot-separator dialect, and make the Stage04 prompt
   state the formal row grammar.

Hansei:
The owner-operation repairs improved typed vocabulary, but a model can still
produce thinner checker-looking syntax if the serialization contract is not
shown as a first-class DSL boundary.

Countermeasure:
The Stage04 prompt now names the exact canonical grammar:
`⟦ACT <bare-ref>[<owner>.<operation>] :: π=<pressure> ::
body_ref=<same-bare-ref> :: Δ=<delta-id>:<delta_result> ::
Land(<burden>)+⟧`, and forbids omitted separators, slot reordering, or
`body_ref=` before `π=`. The runner self-test rejects the Gate66 dialect, and
the staged-runtime-handshake checker now requires the same canonical slot order
for recorded Stage04 rows.

Smoke B command/result:

```powershell
python -m py_compile tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py tools\check_staged_runtime_handshake.py
python tools\check_staged_runtime_handshake.py
python tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py --self-test
```

Result:
PASS. `check_staged_runtime_handshake.py` now reports `12` valid / `76`
invalid fixtures; the new invalid fixture is included in that count. The staged
harness self-test passed and wrote ignored validation artifacts under `.daee/`.

Anti-slimming / non-claims:
This repair does not lower burden, submove, ACT, MRP/NAR, field_witness, or
public-output coverage. It rejects a malformed ACT serialization before Stage05;
it does not make slimmer answers pass. No model smoke rerun, Stage08 sidecar,
retained promotion, package, tag, release, release asset, public sample, or
provenance mutation occurred.

Remaining blocker or closure:
Locally closed for the Secularism Stage04 canonical ACT syntax blocker, pending
the full Smoke B/safety/CI closure gates. TST graph/no-new-resultant and
Trinitarian owner-operation mass remain open Gate66 blockers.

## Gate66C TST Stage07 No-New-Resultant Terminal Proof Repair

Status:
No-model producer/scaffold repair closed and pushed for the Gate66B must-fix
TST false terminal `no_new_resultant` pattern. Source/checker/docs repair commit
`18bc147` reached GitHub CI run `27052075724` success and Pages run
`27052075481` success. No model smoke, failed-case rerun, Stage08 promotion,
retained promotion, package, tag, release, release asset, public sample, or
provenance action occurred.

Scope:
This lane patches only the generic Stage07 scaffold/canary surface in
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`. It does not edit preserved smoke
artifacts. The preserved TST output remains valid negative evidence and still
fails `tools/check_graph_completeness.py` for the original false terminal proof.

Symptom:
The preserved Gate66 TST output held baseline burden `B3` with non-neutral
divergence and unresolved curl, but also emitted a clean
`formal_reread_states[].no_new_resultant_proof.field_state_at_stop` with
`divergence=neutral`, `curl=null`, and `stop_licensed=true`.

Failed check:
`python tools\check_graph_completeness.py --outputs
.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260605-224043-matrix-tst-lillard\output.md
--json` failed with `no_new_resultant_terminal_proof`, `mrp_exhaustion`, and
`curl_loopbreak_handling`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, Stage07 public MRP and
field_witness scaffold generation:
`stage07_mrp_reread_contract_guidance()`,
`stage07_field_witness_contract_guidance()`, and the runner self-test canaries.

Gemba:
- Preserved artifact:
  `.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-224043-matrix-tst-lillard/output.md`
  has `B3: held-with-reason`, `coverage_complete=false`,
  `field_diagnostics.divergence_check=non-neutral / unresolved_burdens=[B3]`,
  and `field_diagnostics.curl_check=unresolved / generated_burden_hold=[B3]`.
- The same artifact also has one `no_new_resultant_proof` whose
  `field_state_at_stop` claims neutral/null STOP closure.
- Existing graph fixtures already reject field-state drift; the missing canary
  was producer-side for a baseline `B_LA` burden held elsewhere in the field,
  not generated `B_MRP` hold accounting.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the symptom occur? The Stage07 scaffold let a later landed burden
   carry a clean `no_new_resultant` STOP row while another baseline burden
   remained held.
2. Why did that condition exist? The STOP normalization checked whether the
   final MRP source itself was unresolved, but not whether any burden in the
   whole selected field remained held/partial.
3. Why did the system allow it? Generated `B_MRP` held branches had self-tests,
   but baseline `B_LA` held branches with empty `B_MRP` did not have an
   equivalent producer-side canary.
4. Why was it not caught before the model matrix? The graph checker caught the
   preserved output after the expensive smoke, but the runner self-test did not
   prove the scaffold would convert a whole-field unresolved STOP into
   HOLD/PARTIAL before the next model call.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add a generic held/partial burden
   detector, make Stage07 MRP and field_witness scaffolds route any false
   whole-field STOP to `hold_partial` / `HOLD` on the actual held burden, and add
   a neutral baseline-held self-test canary.

Hansei:
The prior generated-burden hardening was correct but too narrow. It protected
`B_MRP` holds while leaving a baseline held burden able to be hidden behind a
clean STOP proof on a different burden. The repair preserves burden identity by
moving the terminal source to the held burden instead of allowing a global
neutral proof to stand.

Countermeasure:
- Added `stage07_held_or_partial_burdens()` as a generic structural helper.
- Updated both Stage07 public MRP and field_witness scaffold paths so a
  `no_new_resultant` / `STOP` row is converted to `hold_partial` / `HOLD` when
  any selected burden remains held or unresolved.
- Added a neutral self-test fixture with baseline burdens `B1`, `B2`, `B3`,
  where `B2` is `held-with-reason` and the incoming Stage05 reread attempts a
  clean `B3` STOP. The canary requires `MRP(B2)` / `hold_partial` / `HOLD`,
  non-neutral/unresolved field diagnostics, no terminal STOP proof on the held
  burden, and `coverage_complete=false`.

Interim self-test Andons while adding the canary:
- First self-test failure:
  `Self-test baseline-held MRP prompt omitted scaffold:
  coverage_complete=false; unresolved_burdens=[²B]`. Cause: the assertion
  looked for coverage text in the MRP block instead of the field_witness
  ledger. Countermeasure: assert MRP route fields in the MRP block and
  coverage state in the witness text.
- Second self-test failure:
  `Self-test baseline-held canary omitted B2 formal reread state`. Cause:
  `first_json_object_from_text()` parsed the visible empty set notation
  `B_MRP = {}` before the actual `field_witness` JSON. Countermeasure: parse
  the JSON after the `field_witness` marker for this canary.

## Gate66D Stage07 M8 Owner-Operation Mass Repair

No-model producer/canary repair opened from the Four-Smoke Closure Readiness
Audit. This lane does not run model smokes, does not rerun failed cases, does
not build Stage08 sidecars, does not promote retained proof, and does not
package, tag, release, publish assets, or mutate provenance.

Symptom:
The preserved Gate66 Trinitarian output reached Stage07 output assembly but
failed the manual render contract. The output carried required public markers,
including owner/TTP markers and `Land(⁵B)`, but the M8 consequence-trace public
body did not trace an actual downstream implication/dependency and the Layer B
body mass did not license the landing claim.

Failed check:
`python tools\check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py --outputs
.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260605-224043-matrix-trinitarian-j173\output.md`
failed with:
- `Layer B submove mass insufficient: required fields are present, but
  owner/TTP work is conclusion-shaped rather than operation-shaped`;
- `M8 submove did not trace consequences.`;
- `Land(⁵B) claimed without mass-sufficient Layer B treatment`;
- `Baseline burden treated as if low-mass without diagnostic license`.

Owner file / owner surface:
`tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, Stage07 ACT slice producer guidance
for `stage07_act_contract_guidance()` and its M8 owner-specific self-test;
`tests/manual-smoke-render/invalid/` manual-render fixture surface.

Gemba:
- Preserved artifact:
  `.daee/release-regression-smokes/20260605-224043-matrix-trinitarian-j173/output.md`.
- Current manual-render validator already rejects the preserved artifact, so
  the checker is strict enough to stop release validation.
- The missing pre-matrix guard was a neutral fixture/producerside canary for the
  exact marker-rich/body-thin M8 shape, plus Stage07 scaffold language that
  makes fake `Land(Bn)` less likely before a model call.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the symptom occur? Stage07 public rendering allowed an M8 row with
   owner markers and a controlled delta to stand even though the public body did
   not execute a consequence trace.
2. Why did that condition exist? The scaffold told the model to use
   `consequence-trace`, but did not explicitly say marker-rich rows without a
   concrete if-accepted implication and state change must become HOLD/PARTIAL.
3. Why did the system allow it? Manual validators rejected the final artifact,
   but the no-model fixtures did not isolate this exact M8 body-thin Land shape
   before the expensive matrix.
4. Why was it not caught earlier? Prior M8 canaries covered unregistered
   operations and generic body gaps mostly through MRP-generated-burden
   fixtures, not the public manual-render mass boundary that failed Gate66.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add a neutral invalid manual-render
   fixture for M8 marker-rich/body-thin `Land(Bn)` and strengthen the Stage07 M8
   scaffold/self-test so the public body must state the consequence/dependency,
   the tested if-accepted implication, and the burden-local state change or
   render HOLD/PARTIAL instead of `Land(Bn)`.

Hansei:
The previous owner-operation repairs improved vocabulary and body-backed
checking, but still let the next model spend into a public-rendering failure
where markers looked correct while the operation body was too thin. The repair
must preserve burden richness: it does not lower burden/submove expectations or
make outputs slimmer; it tells the producer to hold/partial when the typed M8
transition is not publicly executed.

Countermeasure:
- Added `tests/manual-smoke-render/invalid/m8-consequence-trace-body-thin-land.md`.
  The fixture is neutral and contains no Trinitarian/TST/Khaybar/Secularism
  prompt identity gate. It proves a controlled `M8.consequence-trace` row with
  a body-thin public paragraph and `Land(Bn)` is invalid.
- Updated `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage07 M8 guidance: a
  marker-rich row is not enough; if the public body cannot state the
  consequence/dependency, tested if-accepted implication, and burden-local state
  change, the producer must render HOLD/PARTIAL instead of `Land(Bn)`.
- Added a runner self-test assertion that the strengthened M8 scaffold remains
  present.

Andon:
Status: matrix failed 0/4. This is not a shared infrastructure stop; the runner
worked, model transport returned, prompt/raw-input handling worked, artifacts
were preserved, and deterministic checkers replayed. The failures are proof
surface failures:
- one Stage03 controlled owner-operation vocabulary failure;
- three Stage07 public projection / witness / graph reconstructibility failures.

Failed checks:
- Secularism Stage03 owner route validation rejected uncontrolled
  `M7.definition-discipline`.
- Trinitarian/TST/Khaybar Stage07 output validation rejected visible governed
  output / NAR / body_ref / field_witness / graph completeness surfaces.

Owner file / owner surface:
- Stage03: `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` owner-route validation and
  `atomics/skill/references/diagnostics/delta-result-vocabulary.json` M7 owner
  operation vocabulary.
- Stage07: `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` public projection guidance
  and Stage07 handoff, `tools/check_manual_smoke_render_contract.py`,
  `tools/check_mrp_generated_burden.py`,
  `tools/check_nla_decode_semantic_faithfulness.py`,
  `tools/check_graph_completeness.py`, and the public output / field_witness /
  formal_reread parity contract.

5 Whys - Stage03 owner-operation vocabulary:
1. Why did Secularism fail? Stage03 emitted `definition-discipline` as an
   executable M7 operation.
2. Why was that invalid? The controlled M7 operation vocabulary allows
   `anchor` and `definition-anchor`, not route/prose labels.
3. Why did the model emit it? The scaffold still permits owner-family purpose
   words to leak into callable-operation position.
4. Why did the previous lane not catch it? Gate66 closed several owner-operation
   families, but not this M7 operation-label negative shape.
5. Countermeasure: add a neutral Stage03 canary for invalid
   `M7.definition-discipline` executable routing and a valid/HOLD fixture for
   `M7.definition-anchor` or non-executable M7 routing.

5 Whys - Stage07 public/witness reconstructibility:
1. Why did three cases fail Stage07? Their public outputs could not be
   reconstructed by manual render, MRP, NLA decode, field_witness, or graph
   completeness validators.
2. Why did that happen after Stage01-06 passed? The staged internal record could
   reach output assembly, but public projection omitted or distorted required
   governed scaffold, body_ref dereferences, field_witness land targets, or
   formal_reread/visible lines.
3. Why did the system allow model spend into this? Existing canaries covered
   slices of body-backed operation, M8 mass, SOURCE transitions, and graph
   completeness, but the producer still lacks a single Stage07 projection
   contract that forces public output to mirror the full typed trace.
4. Why was Khaybar a regression signal? A prior Khaybar run passed, but this
   fresh one failed body_ref/witness parity, proving the issue can repopulate
   even in a case that had previous pass evidence.
5. Countermeasure: open one generic no-model Stage07 projection/witness repair
   lane before any rerun, with neutral fixtures for output scaffold retention,
   exact ACT body_ref-to-Layer-B dereference, field_witness `Land()` parity,
   formal_reread-visible parity, graph witness agreement, and forbidden public
   Graphify/ActiveGraph/T_lang proof claims.

Hansei:
Gate66D correctly closed the M8 operation-mass failure, but the fresh matrix
shows the model can still produce thinner or malformed public projections while
internal stages appear sufficient. The repair path must not lower output burden
or submove richness. It must make Stage07 projection carry the typed DSL/IR
state into visible output rather than treating sidecars, NAR, or internal stage
records as substitutes for public governed execution.

Decision:
`MATRIX_NOT_AUTHORIZED_FOR_STAGE08_OR_RELEASE`

Do not build Stage08 sidecars, do not promote retained proof, do not package,
tag, create a GitHub Release, create assets, mutate provenance, or claim
v0.4.4.0 readiness. Before another model matrix, open a no-model repair loop.

Minimum no-model repair lanes before rerun:
1. Gate67A Stage03 M7 owner-operation availability lane:
   add/verify neutral valid/invalid canaries for controlled M7 operations,
   executable Land permission, and HOLD/PARTIAL behavior when the routed owner
   lacks callable operation/delta mapping.
2. Gate67B Stage07 public projection / field_witness / body_ref parity lane:
   add/verify neutral fixtures covering public scaffold retention, exact
   body_ref dereference, field_witness `Land()` target parity, formal_reread to
   visible `R(H,Delta)` parity, MRP prose placement, graph completeness, and no
   public Graphify/ActiveGraph proof or guaranteed `T_lang` claim.

Non-claims:
- No model smoke rerun.
- No Stage08 sidecar build.
- No docs Grapher proof for these failed outputs.
- No retained promotion.
- No package, tag, GitHub Release, release assets, public sample, or provenance.

from typed proof states instead of letting a fluent output substitute for the
transition path.

Symptom:

- Trinitarian/TST/Khaybar reached output but failed Stage07 because public
  prose, field_witness, NAR, body_ref dereference, Land targets, and
  graph/proof claims did not remain orthogonalized proof states.

Failed check:
- Gate67 Secularism Stage03:
  `operation token 'definition-discipline' is outside controlled operation
  vocabulary for M7`.
- Gate67 Stage07 outputs:
  visible governed output, NLA decode, field_witness convergence, MRP
  generated-burden, manual smoke render, and graph completeness direct replays.

Owner file / owner surface:
- Stage03 owner operation state:
  `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`,
  `tools/check_staged_runtime_handshake.py`, and
  `atomics/skill/references/diagnostics/delta-result-vocabulary.json`.
- Stage07 public projection / witness state:
  `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, especially
  `stage07_field_witness_contract_guidance()`,
  `visible_governed_output_errors()`, and release validator ordering.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the matrix fail again? The model emitted surface-valid-looking
   labels/prose that did not carry distinct hidden proof states through the
   staged runtime.
2. Why were the surface labels insufficient? `definition-discipline`,
   `field_witness`, `NAR`, `Land`, `T_lang`, Graphify, and ActiveGraph each
   have multiple possible proof roles, but only some roles are executable or
   proof-bearing.
3. Why did the system allow the ambiguity to reach model spend? Prior canaries
   rejected many exact bad shapes, but the producer scaffold did not state the
   OSM-style clone-state separation sharply enough at Stage07, and the visible
   gate accepted a `field_witness` heading without confirming a parseable
   machine witness object.
4. Why was this not caught earlier? The direct validators caught the bad
   outputs after model spend, but the no-model suite lacked an exact neutral
   M7 operation-label fixture and a harness self-test for field_witness
   heading-only / missing-NAR / false-proof projection aliases.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add neutral canaries and narrow
   producer/checker wording so route label != operation, heading != witness
   object, witness object != NAR unless structured, sidecar/custody != proof,
   and Land prose != field_witness land_target.

Hansei:
The previous repair lanes closed important owner-operation and SOURCE formal
transition gaps, but the Gate67 outputs prove that public projection can still
be thinner than the typed trace. This lane must not make output slimmer. It
must make the required public scaffold harder to omit and make the visible
validator fail earlier when public state is not reconstructible.

Countermeasure:
- Added neutral invalid Stage03 fixture
  `tests/staged-runtime-handshake/invalid/stage03-m7-route-label-as-operation.json`
  so an M7 route label cannot masquerade as a callable operation.
- Strengthened Stage07 field_witness scaffold wording in
  `tools/run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` to split visible witness,
  machine field_witness JSON, NAR rows, sidecar custody, bare body_ref, and
  Land target mirrors as separate clone states.
- Strengthened `visible_governed_output_errors()` so a field_witness heading
  must be followed by a parseable checker-owned witness object, and that object
  must include structured `normalized_activation_record`.
- Added Stage07 self-test probes rejecting heading-only field_witness,
  missing-NAR field_witness, guaranteed `T_lang` uptake claims, and
  Graphify/ActiveGraph proof claims.

Protected DSL transition:
`burden_id -> route/TTP selection -> owner operation -> ACT row -> controlled
delta_result -> MRP/NAR update -> field_witness mirror -> public output
witness`. The repair specifically protects the Stage03
`owner_id/operation/delta_result` availability boundary and the Stage07
`field_witness/NAR/public projection` boundary.

   public witness object that the checker can reconstruct.
4. Why did existing fixtures not close this exact alias? Gate68 runner

   staged-runtime fixture suite had no neutral Stage07 output fixtures for
   those same aliases.
5. What prevents recurrence? Share the parser-stable field_witness extraction
   predicate in the handoff checker and add neutral invalid Stage07 fixtures for
   heading-only and missing structured NAR states.

checker surface still accepted a surface observation as if it were the hidden
state. The countermeasure must not make outputs thinner. It should make the
same public governed scaffold stricter: `field_witness` text is not enough
unless it carries the structured witness object and its NAR mirror.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the matrix fail? The outputs reached `output.md` but post-output
   validators found ACT delta and formal reread-state semantics that were not
   typed enough for proof.
2. Why did the rows look acceptable to the model? Compact public labels such as
   `D7:...`, `Δ⁵B₂:...`, and `reopen-condition-stated` were treated as if they
   were the same hidden state as a burden-state delta, Delta-kappa transition,

   MRP states through the same visible `source_burden = B5` key without a clone
   state, then failed to mirror the canonical `mrp_resultants[B5]` row.
4. Why was this not caught before the model spend? Existing no-model canaries
   covered body_ref pollution, owner-operation availability, field_witness
   projection, and several token-projection classes, but did not yet cover this
   exact Stage07 ACT Delta-field shape or the duplicate formal-reread source
   clone-state split.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add neutral no-model canaries and
   checker/scaffold repairs that force the typed transition split:
   ACT `Delta` must be a burden-state/Delta-kappa carrier, `delta_result` must
   be a concrete owner-local state change, and `formal_reread_states` must
   mirror `mrp_resultants` one-to-one unless an explicit clone-state schema is
   introduced.

and formal reread/MRP mirroring. The outputs were not thin: B_LA, ACT, and
submove coverage remained substantial, and the failures are stricter-structure
failures rather than evidence that the matrix should accept slimmer prose.

## Gate70A Stage07 ACT Delta Carrier Hidden-State Repair

Boundary:
This is a no-model repair lane. No failed smoke was rerun, no new model smoke
was launched, and no Stage08, retained promotion, package, tag, GitHub Release,
release asset, public sample, provenance, or release-readiness claim is opened
by this record.

| SOURCE `authority-order-repair` and `source-order-repair` formal-transition work | already satisfied/stale for this lane | Gate62H SOURCE work is preserved by current owner/vocabulary/checker surfaces; Gate70A does not reopen it. |
| Compact tokens and public prose are observations, not proof | still open and relevant to this lane | Gate70 TST, Khaybar, and Secularism failed because ACT `Delta` display tokens or suffix labels were treated as if they carried hidden transition evidence. |
| Stricter formalism must reduce fake closure, not thinner outputs | architectural invariant to preserve | Gate70 outputs were large and structurally rich; this patch adds canary coverage and scaffold wording only, with no count-reduction or output-slimming change. |

Andon:
- Symptom: Gate70 TST, Khaybar, and Secularism reached `output.md` but
  failed Stage07 post-output MRP/generated-burden validation because ACT
  `Delta` rows used compact labels as proof carriers.
- Failed check:
  `python tools\check_mrp_generated_burden.py --outputs <Gate70 output.md>
  --show-advisories`.
- Owner file / owner surface:
  Stage04/Stage07 ACT scaffold in
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, runtime source in
  `atomics\skill\SKILL.md`, generated-runtime builder in
  `tools\build_compiled_runtime.py`, and neutral MRP generated-burden
  fixtures.
- Failure class:
  `token/prose/label projection without hidden transition evidence` and
  `latent-state aliasing requiring clone/state split`.

Protected DSL transition:
`burden_id -> owner operation -> ACT row -> Delta carrier
(burden-state or Delta-kappa) -> owner-local delta_result -> MRP/NAR update
-> field_witness mirror -> public output witness`.

OSM/CSCG hidden-state split:
- ACT `Delta` display carrier is one state.
- Owner-local `delta_result` suffix is a second state.
- Burden terminal state is a third state.
- `κ/H` dependency-radius transition is a fourth state.
Gate70A teaches the producer/scaffold that these observations must not be
collapsed into the same carrier token.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the symptom occur? Stage07 public ACT rows used `D7`,
   submove-indexed carriers such as `Δ¹B₁`, or generic labels such as
   `reopen-condition-stated` where the checker required a burden-state or
   Delta-kappa carrier plus concrete state-change suffix.
2. Why did the producer emit those forms? The scaffold said to preserve ACT
   rows but did not explicitly distinguish the carrier before the colon from
   the owner-local `delta_result` after the colon.
3. Why did this become a proof failure? The checker correctly treats
   carrier, owner-local result, terminal state, and dependency-radius state as
   distinct hidden states; the output conflated them in a fluent public token.
4. Why was it not caught earlier? Existing canaries covered generic bad
   carriers and body_ref pollution, but not the Gate70 shapes: diagnostic
   step IDs (`D7`/`D8`), submove-indexed Delta carriers, or a P7 reopen-label
   suffix without a reconstructible state transition.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Add neutral canaries for those
   shapes and patch the source/runtime/stage guidance so ACT `Delta` carrier
   is only `ΔBn`/`ΔⁿB` or `Δκ`, while the suffix remains the owner-local
   state change.

Hansei:
Gate69 closed a public witness projection alias, but Gate70 exposed a deeper
ACT delta-carrier alias. The right repair is not to loosen
`check_mrp_generated_burden.py`, accept more aliases, or move proof into
sidecars; it is to make the scaffold preserve typed transition separation in
the public governed output.

Countermeasure:
- Added ACT delta-layer wording to `atomics\skill\SKILL.md`.
- Added Stage04 and Stage07 scaffold guidance to
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`.
- Added generated-runtime builder wording in
  `tools\build_compiled_runtime.py`, then regenerated `skill\SKILL.md`.
- Added neutral MRP/generated-burden canaries:
  - valid `tests\mrp-generated-burden\valid\act-delta-kappa-carrier-split.md`;
  - invalid
    `tests\mrp-generated-burden\invalid\act-delta-compact-step-id-carrier.md`;

    `tests\mrp-generated-burden\invalid\act-delta-submove-indexed-carrier.md`;
  - invalid
    `tests\mrp-generated-burden\invalid\p7-reopen-condition-label-without-state-change.md`.
- Rebuilt generated runtime and docs from source owners.

This repair does not remove any burden, generated burden, ACT row, MRP/NAR
mirror, field_witness mirror, or public proof scaffold. It adds no-model
coverage and producer guidance so future outputs keep valid work typed rather
than thinner.

## Gate70B CI Control-Plane Size Andon

Boundary:
This is a CI-failure repair for the Gate70A pushed commit
`faa688d10c9ea499cbf1f4adf98f63bfbc53af2d`. It does not rerun a model smoke,
open Stage08, promote retained proof, package, tag, release, publish assets, or
mutate provenance.

Andon:
- Symptom: GitHub CI failed after Gate70A push while local Smoke B and Pages
  had passed.
- Failed check: CI run `27056380450`, job `runtime-checks`, command
  `python tools/check_recursion_collapse_noetic_frame.py`.
- Failing output:
  `recursion collapse / noetic-frame guard: FAIL - atomics/skill/SKILL.md
  appears to have re-expanded beyond control-plane size`.
- Owner file / owner surface:
  root control-plane source `atomics\skill\SKILL.md`; checker
  `tools\check_recursion_collapse_noetic_frame.py`; generated runtime rebuilt
  from `tools\build_compiled_runtime.py`.
- Failure class:
  `checker/normalizer failed to preserve DSL` is not the issue; this is a
  producer/scaffold documentation-placement error where the root control plane
  grew past its CI size guard.

5 Whys:
1. Why did CI fail? The root `atomics\skill\SKILL.md` exceeded the 80,000
   character control-plane ceiling enforced by
   `check_recursion_collapse_noetic_frame.py`.
2. Why did it exceed the ceiling? Gate70A added detailed ACT delta-carrier
   guidance directly to the always-loaded root skill.
3. Why was that the wrong owner surface for detail? The root skill is a compact
   control plane; detailed repair contracts belong in scaffold/generator text
   and neutral canaries unless they are minimal always-loaded invariants.
4. Why was this missed locally? The pre-push Smoke B suite did not include
   `check_recursion_collapse_noetic_frame.py`, while CI does.
5. What prevents recurrence? Keep root wording compact, preserve details in
   `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, `tools\build_compiled_runtime.py`,
   and no-model fixtures, and include the recursion/noetic-frame size guard in
   local release-line Smoke B whenever root `SKILL.md` changes.

Hansei:
The DSL repair was directionally right, but the root-control-plane placement
was too verbose. Compacting root wording is not a weakening move because the
strict evidence lives in the generated scaffold, checker-owned fixture suite,
and existing validators.

Countermeasure:
- Compressed the Gate70A addition in `atomics\skill\SKILL.md` to a minimal

Andon:
- Symptom: Gate71 produced four one-shot smoke failures. Three cases reached
  `output.md` and failed Stage07 post-output validators; Khaybar failed at
  Stage03 owner-route operation vocabulary before `output.md`.
- Failed checks:
  `check_owner_activation_ordering.py` for Trinitarian,
  `check_graph_completeness.py` for TST,
  Stage03 runner owner-operation validation for Khaybar, and
  `check_nla_decode_semantic_faithfulness.py` for Secularism.
- Owner file / owner surface:
  Stage03 owner-route scaffold and SOURCE owner-operation vocabulary;
  Stage07 release-output scaffold/assembler; NLA ACT syntax parser;
  owner activation ordering; graph completeness; MRP/NAR/field_witness/public
  witness projection.
- Failure classes:
  producer/scaffold failed to emit DSL; token/prose/label projection without
  hidden transition evidence; latent-state aliasing requiring clone/state
  split. No transport/infrastructure failure was observed.

5 Whys:
1. Why did the matrix fail? Every case exposed a typed-state mismatch before
   Stage08/retained promotion could open.
2. Why did the outputs or stage handoffs look close? Surface tokens looked
   governed (`Δκ`, `held-with-reason`, SOURCE operation text, public ACT
   fences), but the hidden proof states were not orthogonalized enough for
   validators.
3. Why did this survive no-model preflight? The no-model suite covered the
   Gate70 exact aliases and several structural classes, but did not include
   these fresh variants: `Δκ` without explicit carrier evidence, unresolved
   curl with generated hold closed, prefixed owner-alias operation token in
   Stage03, and public ACT opening rows missing `body_ref=`.
4. Why is this not a release/provenance gate? The failures are proof-surface
   Andons; no case reached verifier-sidecar/retained-promotion eligibility.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Open one no-model repair-planning
   loop, ordered by earliest failing stage and recurrence risk, adding neutral
   canaries before any next model matrix.

Hansei:
Gate70A/B improved the previous ACT delta-carrier failure class, and the
matrix became more informative rather than merely repeating the same errors.
However, the repairs left nearby hidden-state aliases uncovered. The next
repair must avoid broader prose tolerance: each countermeasure needs a
structure-based canary and must preserve public burden/submove richness.

Countermeasure / Kaizen:
Open one no-model repair loop before any new matrix:
1. Stage03 SOURCE owner-operation alias split: reject `owner_alias.operation`
   in `operation` and teach controlled owner-local operation tokens.
2. Stage07 ACT public-fence syntax: every visible `⟦ACT ...⟧` row must include
   canonical owner.operation, `π=`, bare `body_ref=`, `Δ=`, and Land/HOLD
   syntax; no duplicate malformed opening rows.
3. Stage07 `Δκ` carrier evidence: require explicit kappa carrier,
   dependency-radius, and `R(H,Delta)` evidence for Delta-kappa activations.
4. Stage07 graph/MRP open-hold accounting: non-neutral divergence plus
   unresolved curl and `held-with-reason` must remain explicitly open through
   generated-hold, HOLD/PARTIAL/RECURSE, or LoopBreak evidence.

Anti-slimming check:
No repair should reduce B_LA/B_MRP, ACT, submove, MRP/NAR, field_witness, or
public proof scaffold counts unless Gemba proves duplicate, invalid, inert,
source-blocked, held, unsupported, or over-split material. The Gate71 failures
are not evidence for thinner output; they are evidence for stronger typed
transition carry-forward.

Remaining blocker or closure:
`MATRIX_NOT_AUTHORIZED` for any further model matrix, Stage08 sidecar
eligibility, retained promotion, package, tag, GitHub Release, release asset,
public sample, or provenance until the Gate71 Andons are closed by no-model
canaries/repairs, Smoke B and safety scans pass, the repair is committed and
pushed if allowed, and CI/Pages are green.

## Gate71A Stage03 SOURCE Owner-Operation Alias Repair

Boundary:
This is a no-model repair lane for the earliest Gate71 failure surface. It
does not run a model smoke, rerun Khaybar, open Stage08, promote retained proof,
package, tag, create a GitHub Release, mutate release assets, publish samples,
or mutate provenance.

Re-anchor:
- HEAD / origin/main at lane start:
  `7ffbd3385dfe9f7625b1d088c127b4719613abfe`.
- Worktree: tracked clean before patching.
- CI before patch: PASS, run `27057477665`.
- Pages before patch: PASS, run `27057477332`.

Gemba:
- Preserved artifact:
  `.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-khaybar\responses\stage-03-routing-owner-gate.response.txt`.
- Failure record:
  `.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-khaybar\records\staged-handoff-failure.json`.
- Exact failure:
  `stage-03 owner_routes[0]: operation token 'source-status-repair.source-order' is outside controlled operation vocabulary for SOURCE; allowed: authority-order-repair, sort, source-order, source-order-repair, status`.
- Owner/source inspected:
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`,
  `tools\delta_result_vocabulary.py`,
  `atomics\skill\references\diagnostics\delta-result-vocabulary.json`,
  `tests\staged-runtime-handshake\invalid\stage03-*.json`.

Andon:
- Symptom: Gate71 Khaybar failed at Stage03 before `output.md` because the
  model emitted a display-style owner alias plus operation string in the
  `operation` field.
- Failed check: Stage03 owner-route vocabulary validation inside
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`, backed by
  `route_owner_vocabulary_errors`.
- Owner file / owner surface: Stage03 producer scaffold in
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`; controlled SOURCE owner-operation
  vocabulary in `delta-result-vocabulary.json`.
- Failure class: producer/scaffold failed to emit or expose the DSL
  owner-operation/delta availability required for Stage04 execution.

5 Whys:
1. Why did Khaybar stop at Stage03? The `operation` value was
   `source-status-repair.source-order`, which is not an owner-local SOURCE
   operation token.
2. Why did the model emit that shape? The Stage03 scaffold allowed
   "controlled owner.operation pair" wording but did not explicitly separate
   the canonical `owner_id` field from the owner-local `operation` field.
3. Why was the checker right to reject it? Accepting the prefixed token would
   merge owner alias, ACT display token, and callable operation, violating the
   DSL layer separation.
4. Why was this not caught before the matrix? Existing canaries rejected
   route-label and mode-label operation tokens, but there was no neutral
   Stage03 canary for `owner_alias.operation` inside the `operation` field.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Keep the strict vocabulary helper,
   add Stage03 scaffold guidance for owner-local operation fields, and add a
   neutral invalid fixture plus runner self-test for the prefixed alias shape.

Hansei:
The prior no-model readiness suite had the right strict checker surface but
missed a near-neighbor producer ambiguity. The repair must not normalize or
accept polluted operation strings; it should make the scaffold carry the typed
state split before Stage04.

Countermeasure:
- Updated `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage03 instructions:
  `owner_id` carries the owner/approved alias; `operation` /
  `owner_operation` carries only the owner-local callable operation token.
- Added a runner self-test rejecting
  `operation: source-status-repair.source-order`.
- Added neutral invalid fixture
  `tests\staged-runtime-handshake\invalid\stage03-prefixed-owner-operation-token.json`.

5 Whys:
1. Why did Secularism fail Stage07? The public opening emitted two ACT fences
   missing `body_ref=`.
2. Why did they appear before Layer B? The visible opening role forbade Layer B
   but did not explicitly forbid ACT-shaped summary fences outside the ACT
   partition.
3. Why did the checker reject them? In this DSL, every visible `⟦ACT ...⟧`
   fence is a proof-bearing row and must carry owner.operation, pressure,
   bare `body_ref=`, delta, and Land/HOLD state.
4. Why was this not caught before Gate71? Existing NLA fixtures covered
   malformed/mirrored ACT rows, but there was no neutral fixture where a
   non-ACT opening preview contained a malformed ACT fence while the later
   canonical row was otherwise valid.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Make ACT-fence syntax a global
   Stage07 compiled-output invariant and add a neutral invalid NLA canary for
   opening ACT fences missing `body_ref=`.

Hansei:
The repair must not make output thinner or hide the public proof scaffold.
Opening prose may preview burdens, but it cannot use ACT syntax unless it is an
actual canonical Stage04-derived ACT row. This preserves public governed
execution while blocking malformed summary rows.

Countermeasure:
- Updated the compiled Stage07 prompt boundary: outside `layer_b_act` sections,
  do not emit any line beginning with `⟦ACT`; inside `layer_b_act`, every
  visible ACT row must be copied from Stage04 and include `body_ref=`.
- Updated the visible-opening role to forbid any `⟦ACT` fence and use ordinary
  prose/bullets for previews.
- Added runner self-test assertions for the opening and ACT-slice prompt text.
- Added neutral invalid fixture
  `tests\nla-decode-semantic-faithfulness\invalid\opening-act-fence-missing-body-ref.md`.

## Gate71C Stage07 Delta-Kappa Carrier Evidence Repair

Boundary:
This is a no-model repair lane for the Gate71 Trinitarian Stage07 owner
activation ordering failure. It does not rerun Trinitarian, run a model smoke,
open Stage08, promote retained proof, package, tag, create a GitHub Release,
mutate release assets, publish samples, or mutate provenance.

Re-anchor:
- HEAD / origin/main at lane start:
  `759e69c011a9ae4520537d0d10539f58efde4955`.
- Worktree: tracked clean before patching except this lane's source/fixture
  edits.
- CI before patch: PASS, run `27058168915`.
- Pages before patch: PASS, run `27058168759`.

Gemba:
- Preserved artifact:
  `.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-trinitarian-j173\output.md`.
- Exact replay:
  `python tools\check_owner_activation_ordering.py --require-plan --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-trinitarian-j173\output.md`
  failed on `owner_activations[5]`.
- Checker error:
  `Delta-kappa without raw burden target requires explicit kappa carrier/dependency-radius/R(H,Delta) evidence`.
- Failed field-witness mirror:
  `body_ref=³B₁`, `owner=M8`, `operation=consequence-trace`,
  `delta=Δκ:entailment-blocked`, `target=B3`, `terminal_state=landed`,
  with no `kappa_carrier`, `dependency_radius`, or
  `reread_state_effect`.
- Owner/source inspected:
  `tools\check_owner_activation_ordering.py`,
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`,
  existing `tests\owner-activation-ordering\valid\kappa-delta-with-carrier.md`,
  existing `tests\owner-activation-ordering\invalid\kappa-delta-without-carrier.md`,
  and the preserved Trinitarian Stage07 artifact.

Andon:
- Symptom: Stage07 public output contained visible `Δκ` / `R(H,Δ)` evidence,
  but the machine field_witness `owner_activations[]` mirror landed an M8
  consequence-trace transition without the explicit hidden-state carrier fields
  required to reconstruct the dependency-radius transition.
- Failed check: `check_owner_activation_ordering.py --require-plan`.
- Owner file / owner surface: Stage07 `field_witness` scaffold in
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`; owner-activation ordering
  fixture surface.
- Failure class: token/prose/label projection without hidden transition
  evidence; producer/scaffold failed to emit the DSL mirror required by the
  existing checker.

5 Whys:
1. Why did Trinitarian fail Stage07? The field_witness owner activation used a
   `Δκ` carrier while landing the burden but omitted the required kappa carrier
   evidence fields.
2. Why are those fields required? `Δκ` is a dependency-radius / collapse-radius
   transition, not a raw burden delta by itself; it must remain reconstructible
   through carrier, dependency-radius, and `R(H,Delta)` evidence.
3. Why did the producer omit them? The Stage07 scaffold mirrored ordinary ACT
   fields but did not add extra required mirror fields when the ACT delta
   carrier was `Δκ` / Delta-kappa.
4. Why did existing no-model coverage miss this producer gap? Generic valid and
   invalid kappa-delta fixtures already enforced the checker rule, but they did
   not cover M8 `consequence-trace` scaffold emission from Stage04 act row
   details into Stage07 field_witness.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Keep the checker strict, add
   Stage07 scaffold instructions and self-test coverage for `Δκ` mirrors, and
   add neutral M8 valid/invalid fixtures proving carrier evidence is required
   for landed kappa-delta owner activations.

Hansei:
The failure was not cosmetic and should not be repaired by accepting thinner
field_witness mirrors. Public prose already showed dependency work; the
machine mirror failed to carry the same hidden-state transition. The
countermeasure must preserve the whole public proof scaffold while making the
typed mirror explicit enough for reconstruction.

Countermeasure:
- Updated `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage07 field_witness
  guidance: every `owner_activations[]` mirror whose delta carrier is `Δκ` /
  Delta-kappa must include `kappa_carrier`, `dependency_radius`, and
  `reread_state_effect`.
- Updated the Stage07 mirror scaffold so generated examples for `Δκ` ACT rows
  include those three fields while keeping `body_ref` bare.
- Added runner self-test assertions that an M8 `consequence-trace` `Δκ` row
  produces the required scaffold fields.
- Added neutral fixtures:
  `tests\owner-activation-ordering\valid\m8-kappa-delta-with-carrier.md` and
  `tests\owner-activation-ordering\invalid\m8-kappa-delta-without-carrier.md`.

## Gate71D Stage07 Graph B_LA Hold-Open State Split

Boundary:
This is a no-model repair lane for the Gate71 TST Stage07 graph-completeness
failure. It does not rerun TST, run a model smoke, open Stage08, promote
retained proof, package, tag, create a GitHub Release, mutate release assets,
publish samples, or mutate provenance.

Re-anchor:
- HEAD / origin/main at lane start:
  `4bf13872fcf284d396147e1aa2bb25e6e0216912`.
- Worktree: tracked clean before patching.
- CI before patch: PASS, run `27058558990`.
- Pages before patch: PASS, run `27058558668`.

Gemba:
- Preserved artifact:
  `.daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-tst-lillard\output.md`.
- Exact replay:
  `python tools\check_graph_completeness.py --outputs .daee\release-regression-smokes\20260606-034946-gate71-matrix-tst-lillard\output.md`
  failed with `graph-completeness failures=['mrp_exhaustion',
  'curl_loopbreak_handling']`.
- Field witness evidence: `B3` is in `B_LA`, `B_MRP` is empty,
  `terminal_states.B3=held-with-reason`, formal reread state uses
  `route_result_type=hold_partial`, `route=HOLD`, `divergence_state=non-neutral`,
  and `curl_state=unresolved`.
- Latent-state alias evidence: the TST output's curl diagnostic used
  `generated_burden_hold=[B3]` even though `B3` was not a generated burden.
- Owner/source inspected:
  `tools\check_graph_completeness.py`,
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`,
  `tests\graph-completeness\invalid\tst-generated-held-false-complete.md`,
  `tests\graph-completeness\invalid\tst-generated-held-open-nonpositive.md`,
  and the preserved TST Stage07 artifact.

Andon:
- Symptom: a baseline `B_LA` burden was intentionally held open, but the
  Stage07 field diagnostics used the generated-burden curl label and the graph
  checker only recognized generated open-hold states as valid non-collapse
  open states.
- Failed check: `check_graph_completeness.py` `mrp_exhaustion` and
  `curl_loopbreak_handling`.
- Owner file / owner surface: Stage07 field diagnostics scaffold in
  `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py`; schema-light graph-completeness
  open-state predicates in `tools\check_graph_completeness.py`.
- Failure class: latent-state aliasing requiring clone/state split; token/label
  projection without correct hidden transition state.

5 Whys:
1. Why did TST fail Stage07? `B3` remained held-with-reason with non-neutral
   divergence and unresolved curl, so the checker correctly refused
   collapse-positive graph completion.
2. Why was this not simply generated-hold accounting? `B3` belonged to
   `B_LA`, not `B_MRP`; it was an initial-floor held burden, not a generated
   MRP resultant.
3. Why did the producer make that ambiguous? The Stage07 scaffold emitted
   `generated_burden_hold=[...]` for all unresolved burdens rather than
   splitting generated MRP holds from baseline `B_LA` holds.
4. Why did the checker not have the split? It already recognized explicit
   generated open-hold states, but lacked a sibling predicate for explicit
   `B_LA` hold-open states backed by formal HOLD/PARTIAL reread evidence.
5. What countermeasure prevents recurrence? Split the field-diagnostic surface
   and checker hidden state: generated unresolved burdens keep
   `generated_burden_hold=[...]`; initial-floor held burdens use
   `b_la_hold_open=[...]` and pass only with terminal, diagnostics, and formal
   HOLD/PARTIAL evidence.

Hansei:
This repair must not make incomplete output look complete. The correct state is
valid-open, not collapse-positive: coverage remains false, curl canonicalizes
to held only for certificate representation, and Stage08/release eligibility
remains closed while a `B_LA` burden is held open.

Countermeasure:
- Updated `tools\run_staged_current_skill_smoke.py` Stage07 field diagnostics
  generation to emit `generated_burden_hold=[...]` only for `B_MRP` generated
  unresolved burdens and `b_la_hold_open=[...]` for unresolved initial
  `B_LA` burdens.
- Added runner self-test coverage requiring baseline-held output to include
  `b_la_hold_open=[B2]` and reject `generated_burden_hold=[B2]` for a
  non-generated burden.
- Added `explicit_b_la_hold_open` to `tools\check_graph_completeness.py`.
  It accepts a `B_LA` open-hold state only when `coverage_complete=false`,
  terminal states mark the `B_LA` burden live/held, field diagnostics name
  `b_la_hold_open`, and formal reread states carry HOLD/PARTIAL evidence.
- Kept generated and baseline open-hold states non-collapse-positive.

- 5 Whys: the scan failed because the pattern was broader than its stated

  words as secret indicators; the process allowed it because this docs-only
  commit lane used an ad hoc scan; it was caught before commit by staged-only
  execution; the countermeasure is to rerun with credential-shaped tokens only.
- Hansei: safety scans must not create noise that hides real credential risk.

  `secret/token/password`, bearer, OpenAI, GitHub, and private-key shapes only.
